# Graph Report - CAOS  (2026-09-23)

## Corpus Check
- 1254 files · ~2,261,055 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 30458 nodes · 59757 edges · 1404 communities (1134 shown, 231 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 88% EXTRACTED · 12% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 7233 edges (avg confidence: 0.88)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `e04b6538`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
